```
    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    19→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    20→        "file": member.get("file"),
    21→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    22→        "status": member.get("status"),
    23→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    24→    }
    25→
    26→
    27→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    28→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    29→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    30→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    31→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    32→        serialized_members = [
    33→            _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    34→            for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    35→        ]
    36→        member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    37→        serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    38→            "id": item.get("id"),
    39→            "kind": "cluster",
    40→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    41→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    42→            "member_count": member_count,
    43→            "members": serialized_members,
    44→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    45→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    46→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    47→        }
    48→        if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    49→            serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    50→            serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    51→        serialized_cluster["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    52→        return serialized_cluster
    53→
    54→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    55→        "id": item.get("id"),
    56→        "kind": item.get("kind", "issue"),
    57→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    58→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    59→        "file": item.get("file"),
    60→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    61→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    62→        "status": item.get("status"),
    63→    }
    64→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    65→
    66→    # Workflow dependency state

    68→        serialized["blocked_by"] = item["blocked_by"]
    69→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    70→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    71→
    72→    explain = item.get("explain")
    73→    if explain is not None:

    75→

    78→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    79→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    80→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]

    88→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    89→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    90→
    91→    return serialized
    92→
    93→
    94→def build_query_payload(
    95→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    96→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    97→    *,
    98→    command: str,
    99→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   100→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   101→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   102→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   103→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   104→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   105→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   106→    }
   107→
   108→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   109→        "command": command,
   110→        "items": serialized,
   111→        "queue": queue_section,
   112→        "narrative": narrative,
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    if plan and (
   116→        plan.get("queue_order")
   117→        or plan.get("skipped")
   118→        or plan.get("clusters")
   119→    ):
   120→        clusters_summary = []
   121→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   122→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   123→            clusters_summary.append({
   124→                "name": name,
   125→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   126→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   127→            })
   128→        payload["plan"] = {
   129→            "active": True,
   130→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   131→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   132→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   133→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   134→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   135→        }
   136→
   137→    return payload
   138→
   139→
   140→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   141→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   142→    lines = [
   143→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   144→        "",
   145→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   146→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   147→    ]
   148→    for item in items:
   149→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   150→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   151→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   152→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   153→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   154→    lines.append("")
   155→    return "\n".join(lines)
   156→
   157→
   158→def write_output_file(
   159→    output_file: str,
   160→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   161→    item_count: int,
   162→    *,
   163→    safe_write_text_fn,
   164→    colorize_fn,
   165→) -> bool:
   166→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   167→    try:
   168→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   169→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   170→    except OSError as exc:
   171→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   172→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   173→        return False
   174→    return True
   175→
   176→
   177→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   178→    output_format: str,
   179→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   180→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   181→) -> bool:
   182→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   183→    renderers = {
   184→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   185→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   186→    }
   187→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   188→    if renderer is None:
   189→        return False
   190→    renderer()
   191→    return True
   192→
   193→
   194→__all__ = [
   195→    "build_query_payload",
   196→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   197→    "render_markdown",
   198→    "serialize_item",
   199→    "write_output_file",
   200→]
   201→
```

```
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (

    16→)

    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Dimension key normalization
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    36→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    37→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    38→    target = cleaned.lower()
    39→
    40→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    41→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    42→            return str(dim_key)
    43→    return slugify(cleaned)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    47→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    48→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    49→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    50→    return {
    51→        cleaned.lower(),
    52→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    53→        slugify(cleaned),
    54→        canonical.lower(),
    55→        slugify(canonical),
    56→    }
    57→
    58→
    59→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→# Subjective strict scores
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→
    63→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    64→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    65→    if not dim_scores:
    66→        return {}
    67→
    68→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    69→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    70→    for entry in entries:
    71→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    72→        if not name:
    73→            continue
    74→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    75→        [REDACTED](name)
    76→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    77→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    78→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    79→            if not key:
    80→                continue
    81→            aliases.add(key)
    82→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    83→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    84→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    85→        for alias in aliases:
    86→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    87→    return scores
    88→

    93→
    94→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    95→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
    96→
    97→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
    98→    """
    99→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   100→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   101→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   102→    )
   103→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   104→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   105→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→    order_set = set(order)
   110→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   111→    if not present:
   112→        return []
   113→
   114→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   115→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   116→
   117→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→    open_review_count = sum(
   119→        1 for f in issues.values()
   120→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   121→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   125→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   126→
   127→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   128→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   129→        if sid not in present:
   130→            continue
   131→        if name in confirmed:
   132→            continue
   133→
   134→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   135→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   136→        blocked_by = sorted(
   137→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   138→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   142→        if name == "commit":
   143→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   144→
   145→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   146→            "id": sid,
   147→            "tier": 1,
   148→            "confidence": "high",
   149→            "detector": "triage",
   150→            "file": ".",
   151→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   152→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   153→            "detail": {
   154→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   155→                "stage": name,
   156→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   157→            },
   158→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   159→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   160→        }
   161→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   162→        items.append(item)
   163→    return items
   164→
   165→
   166→def build_subjective_items(
   167→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   168→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   169→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   170→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   171→    if not dim_scores:
   172→        return []
   173→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   174→
   175→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   176→    if not subjective_entries:
   177→        return []
   178→    unassessed_dims = {
   179→        str(name).strip()
   180→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   181→            dim_scores
   182→        )
   183→    }
   184→
   185→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   186→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   187→    for issue in issues.values():
   188→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   189→            continue
   190→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   191→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   192→            if dim_key:
   193→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   194→
   195→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   196→    def _prepare_command(
   197→        cli_keys: list[str],
   198→        *,
   199→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   200→    ) -> str:
   201→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   202→        if cli_keys:
   203→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   204→        if force_review_rerun:
   205→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   206→        return command
   207→
   208→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   209→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   210→        if not name:
   211→            continue
   212→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   213→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   214→            continue
   215→
   216→        [REDACTED](name)
   217→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   218→        cli_keys = [
   219→            str(key).strip().lower()
   220→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   221→            if str(key).strip()
   222→        ]
   223→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   224→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   225→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   226→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   227→            name in unassessed_dims
   228→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   229→        )
   230→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   231→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   232→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   233→        if open_review > 0:
   234→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   235→        else:
   236→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   237→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   238→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   239→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   240→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   241→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   242→            "file": ".",
   243→            "confidence": "medium",
   244→            "summary": summary,
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "dimension_name": name,
   247→                "dimension": dim_key,
   248→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   249→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   250→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   251→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   252→            },
   253→            "status": "open",
   254→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   255→        }
   256→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   257→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   258→        items.append(item)
   259→    return items
```

```
1→"""Parallel execution and progress-callback helpers for review batches."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import threading
     8→from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→
    13→from ._runner_parallel_execution import (
    14→    _drain_parallel_completions,
    15→    _execute_serial,
    16→    _queue_parallel_tasks,
    17→    _resolve_parallel_runtime,
    18→)
    19→from ._runner_parallel_progress import (
    20→    _coerce_batch_execution_options,
    21→)
    22→from ._runner_parallel_types import (
    23→    BatchExecutionOptions,
    24→    BatchProgressEvent,
    25→    BatchResult,
    26→    BatchTask,
    27→)
    28→from .runner_process import _extract_payload_from_log
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→def execute_batches(
    34→    *,
    35→    tasks: dict[int, BatchTask],
    36→    options: BatchExecutionOptions | None = None,
    37→    progress_fn=None,
    38→    error_log_fn=None,
    39→) -> list[int]:
    40→    """Run indexed tasks and return failed index list.
    41→
    42→    Each value in *tasks* is a zero-arg callable returning an int exit code.
    43→    All domain knowledge (files, prompts, etc.) is pre-bound by the caller.
    44→    """
    45→    resolved_options = _coerce_batch_execution_options(options)
    46→    contract_cache: dict[int, str] = {}
    47→    indexes = sorted(tasks)
    48→    if resolved_options.run_parallel:
    49→        max_workers, heartbeat = _resolve_parallel_runtime(
    50→            indexes=indexes,
    51→            max_parallel_workers=resolved_options.max_parallel_workers,
    52→            heartbeat_seconds=resolved_options.heartbeat_seconds,
    53→        )
    54→        failures: set[int] = set()
    55→        progress_failures: set[int] = set()
    56→        started_at: dict[int, float] = {}
    57→        lock = threading.Lock()
    58→        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
    59→            futures = _queue_parallel_tasks(
    60→                executor=executor,
    61→                indexes=indexes,
    62→                tasks=tasks,
    63→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    64→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    65→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    66→                max_workers=max_workers,
    67→                failures=failures,
    68→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    69→                started_at=started_at,
    70→                lock=lock,
    71→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    72→            )
    73→            pending = set(futures.keys())
    74→            _drain_parallel_completions(
    75→                pending=pending,
    76→                futures=futures,
    77→                heartbeat=heartbeat,
    78→                indexes=indexes,
    79→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    80→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    81→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    82→                failures=failures,
    83→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    84→                started_at=started_at,
    85→                lock=lock,
    86→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    87→            )
    88→        return sorted(failures)
    89→    return _execute_serial(
    90→        tasks=tasks,
    91→        indexes=indexes,
    92→        progress_fn=progress_fn,
    93→        error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    94→        clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    95→        contract_cache=contract_cache,
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→def collect_batch_results(
   100→    *,
   101→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   102→    failures: list[int],
   103→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   104→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   105→    extract_payload_fn,
   106→    normalize_result_fn,
   107→) -> tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]:
   108→    """Parse and normalize batch outputs, preserving prior failures."""
   109→    batch_results: list[BatchResult] = []
   110→    failure_set = set(failures)
   111→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   112→        had_execution_failure = idx in failure_set
   113→        raw_path = output_files[idx]
   114→        payload = None
   115→        parsed_from_log = False
   116→        if raw_path.exists():
   117→            try:
   118→                payload = extract_payload_fn(raw_path.read_text())
   119→            except OSError as exc:
   120→                logger.debug("Failed reading batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   121→                payload = None
   122→        if payload is None:
   123→            payload = _extract_payload_from_log(idx, raw_path, extract_payload_fn)
   124→            parsed_from_log = payload is not None
   125→        if payload is None:
   126→            failure_set.add(idx)
   127→            continue
   128→        if parsed_from_log:
   129→            try:
   130→                safe_write_text(raw_path, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   131→            except OSError as exc:
   132→                logger.debug("Failed writing normalized batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   133→        try:
   134→            assessments, issues, dimension_notes, quality = normalize_result_fn(
   135→                payload,
   136→                allowed_dims,
   137→            )
   138→        except ValueError as exc:
   139→            logger.debug("Invalid batch payload at index %s (%s): %s", idx, raw_path, exc)
   140→            failure_set.add(idx)
   141→            continue
   142→        if had_execution_failure:
   143→            failure_set.discard(idx)
   144→        batch_results.append(
   145→            BatchResult(
   146→                batch_index=idx + 1,
   147→                assessments=assessments,
   148→                dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   149→                issues=issues,
   150→                quality=quality,
   151→            )
   152→        )
   153→    return batch_results, sorted(failure_set)
   154→
   155→
   156→__all__ = [
   157→    "BatchResult",
   158→    "BatchExecutionOptions",
   159→    "BatchProgressEvent",
   160→    "collect_batch_results",
   161→    "execute_batches",
   162→]
   163→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any, cast
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    10→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    13→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
    14→    detail_dict,
    15→    is_review_issue,
    16→    is_subjective_issue,
    17→    workflow_stage_name,
    18→    primary_command_for_issue,
    19→    review_issue_weight,
    20→    scope_matches,
    21→    slugify,
    22→    status_matches,
    23→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking_output import (
    26→    group_queue_items,
    27→    item_explain,
    28→    subjective_score_value,
    29→)
    30→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    31→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    32→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    33→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_issues
    34→
    35→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    36→
    37→# Plan-aware sort tiers (item_sort_key)
    38→_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
    39→_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
    40→_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
    41→
    42→# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
    43→_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
    44→_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2    # Epic triage workflow stages
    45→_RANK_WORKFLOW = -1         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
    46→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered issues
    47→_RANK_ISSUE = 1           # Individual issues + assessed subjective
    48→
    49→_TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER = {
    50→    "observe": 0,
    51→    "reflect": 1,
    52→    "organize": 2,
    53→    "commit": 3,
    54→}
    55→
    56→
    57→def _workflow_stage_index(item: WorkQueueItem) -> int:
    58→    raw_index = item.get("stage_index")
    59→    if raw_index is not None:
    60→        try:
    61→            return int(raw_index)
    62→        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
    63→            logger.debug("Invalid workflow stage index %r: %s", raw_index, exc)
    64→    return _TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER.get(workflow_stage_name(item).lower(), 0)
    65→
    66→
    67→def enrich_with_impact(
    68→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    71→
    72→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    73→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    74→    """
    75→    if not dimension_scores:
    76→        for item in items:
    77→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    81→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    82→
    83→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    84→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    85→    for entry in entries:
    86→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    87→        if not name:
    88→            continue
    89→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    90→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    91→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    92→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    93→
    94→    for item in items:
    95→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    96→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    97→
    98→
    99→def _compute_item_impact(
   100→    item: WorkQueueItem,
   101→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
   102→    get_dimension_for_detector,
   103→) -> float:
   104→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
   105→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   106→
   107→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective issues):
   108→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
   109→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   110→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
   111→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
   112→        if entry:
   113→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   114→        return 0.0
   115→
   116→    # Mechanical issues: use detector -> dimension mapping
   117→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   118→    if detector:
   119→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   120→        if dimension:
   121→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
   122→            if entry:
   123→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   124→
   125→    return 0.0
   126→
   127→
   128→def build_issue_items(
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→    *,
   131→    scan_path: str | None,
   132→    status_filter: str,
   133→    scope: str | None,
   134→    chronic: bool,
   135→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   136→    scoped = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   137→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   138→    out: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   139→
   140→    for issue_id, issue in scoped.items():
   141→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
   142→            continue
   143→        if not status_matches(issue.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   144→            continue
   145→        if chronic and not (
   146→            issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   147→        ):
   148→            continue
   149→
   150→        # Evidence-only: skip issues below standalone confidence threshold
   151→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
   152→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   153→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   154→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   155→            issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   156→            if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
   157→                continue
   158→
   159→        item = cast(WorkQueueItem, dict(issue))
   160→        item["id"] = issue_id
   161→        item["kind"] = "issue"
   162→        item["is_review"] = is_review_issue(item)
   163→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_issue(item)
   164→        item["review_weight"] = (
   165→            review_issue_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   166→        )
   167→        subjective_score = None
   168→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   169→            detail = detail_dict(issue)
   170→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   171→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   172→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   173→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   174→            )
   175→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   176→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   177→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_issue(
   178→            item,
   179→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   180→        )
   181→
   182→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   183→            continue
   184→        out.append(item)
   185→
   186→    return out
   187→
   188→
   189→def _natural_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
   190→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   191→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   192→
   193→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority
   194→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   195→        return (_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   196→
   197→    # Triage stage items: stage order, blocked after unblocked
   198→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   199→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   200→        stage_index = _workflow_stage_index(item)
   201→        return (_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   202→
   203→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan)
   204→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   205→        return (_RANK_WORKFLOW, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   206→
   207→    if kind == "cluster":
   208→        # Clusters sort before individual issues, ordered by action type
   209→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   210→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   211→        )
   212→        return (
   213→            _RANK_CLUSTER,
   214→            action_pri,
   215→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   216→            item.get("id", ""),
   217→        )
   218→
   219→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   220→
   221→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   222→        return (
   223→            _RANK_ISSUE,
   224→            -impact,
   225→            subjective_score_value(item),
   226→            item.get("id", ""),
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   230→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   231→    return (
   232→        _RANK_ISSUE,
   233→        -impact,
   234→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   235→        -review_weight,
   236→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   237→        item.get("id", ""),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→def item_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
   242→    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.
   243→
   244→    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
   245→    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
   246→    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.
   247→
   248→    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
   249→    """
   250→    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")
   251→
   252→    if plan_pos is not None:
   253→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   254→        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
   255→        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
   256→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   257→            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   258→            stage_idx = _workflow_stage_index(item)
   259→            return (_TIER_PLANNED, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
   260→        return (_TIER_PLANNED, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   261→
   262→    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
   263→    group = _TIER_NEW if is_new else _TIER_EXISTING
   264→    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
   265→
   266→
   267→__all__ = [
   268→    "build_issue_items",
   269→    "enrich_with_impact",
   270→    "item_explain",
   271→    "item_sort_key",
   272→    "subjective_score_value",
   273→    "group_queue_items",
   274→]
   275→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
```

```
     8→def add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
     9→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
    10→        "plan",
    11→        help="Living plan: generate, show, resolve, skip, cluster, triage",
    12→        description="""\
    13→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
    14→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-issue annotations.
    15→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
    16→        epilog="""\
    17→typical workflow:
    18→  desloppify scan                       # detect issues
    19→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
    20→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
    21→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related issues
    22→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
    23→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
    24→  desloppify plan resolve <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
    25→
    26→patterns (used by reorder, skip, resolve, describe, note, etc.):
    27→  Patterns match issues by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
    28→  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
    29→  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"
    30→
    31→subcommands:
    32→  show       Show plan metadata summary
    33→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
    34→  reset      Reset plan to empty
    35→  reorder    Reposition issues or clusters in the queue
    36→  resolve    Mark issues as fixed (score movement + next-step)
    37→  describe   Set augmented description
    38→  note       Set note on issues
    39→  skip       Skip issues (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
    40→  unskip     Bring skipped issues back to queue
    41→  reopen     Reopen resolved issues
    42→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
    43→  cluster    Manage issue clusters
    44→  triage     Staged triage workflow (after review)""",
    45→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    46→    )

    78→examples:

    81→  desloppify plan reorder smells bottom                        # deprioritize
    82→  desloppify plan reorder my-cluster top                       # cluster members
    83→  desloppify plan reorder my-cluster unused top                # mix clusters + issues
    84→  desloppify plan reorder unused before -t security            # before a issue/cluster
    85→  desloppify plan reorder smells after -t my-cluster           # after a cluster

   306→        parser.add_argument(

   313→        parser.add_argument(

   318→        )
   319→        parser.add_argument(

   325→        parser.add_argument(

   347→  desloppify plan commit-log record --only "smells::*"
   348→  desloppify plan commit-log history             # show commit records
   349→  desloppify plan commit-log pr                  # print PR body markdown""",
   350→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,

   362→
```

```
   198→    def _report_progress(
   199→        progress_event: BatchProgressEvent,
   200→    ) -> None:
   201→        batch_index = progress_event.batch_index
   202→        event = progress_event.event
   203→        code = progress_event.code
   204→        details = progress_event.details
   205→        if event == "heartbeat":
   206→            active = details.get("active_batches")
   207→            queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   208→            elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   209→            if not isinstance(active, list):
   210→                active = []
   211→            if not isinstance(queued, list):
   212→                queued = []
   213→            if not active and not queued:
   214→                return
   215→            segments: list[str] = []
   216→            for idx in active[:6]:
   217→                secs = 0
   218→                if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   219→                    raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   220→                    secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   221→                segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   222→            if len(active) > 6:
   223→                segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   224→            queued_segment = ""
   225→            if queued:
   226→                queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}"
   227→            print(
   228→                colorize_fn(
   229→                    "  Batch heartbeat: "
   230→                    f"{len(active)}/{total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   231→                    f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   232→                    "dim",
   233→                )
   234→            )
   235→            append_run_log(
   236→                "heartbeat "
   237→                f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   238→                f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}"
   239→            )
   240→            if stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   241→                slow_active = [
   242→                    idx
   243→                    for idx in active
   244→                    if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   245→                    and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= stall_warning_seconds
   246→                ]
   247→                newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   248→                if newly_warned:
   249→                    stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   250→                    warning_message = (
   251→                        "  Stall warning: batches "
   252→                        f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   253→                        f"{stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   254→                        "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   255→                    )
   256→                    print(colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   257→                    append_run_log(
   258→                        "stall-warning "
   259→                        f"threshold={stall_warning_seconds}s batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   260→                    )
   261→            return

   291→                    "dim",
   292→                )
   293→            )
   294→            append_run_log(f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   295→            return
   296→        if event == "done":
   297→            status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   298→            tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   299→            elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   300→            elapsed_suffix = ""
   301→            if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   302→                elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   303→                state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   304→            state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   305→            state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   306→            state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   307→            if batch_index in stall_warned_batches:
   308→                stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   309→            print(
   310→                colorize_fn(
   311→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   312→                    tone,
   313→                )
   314→            )
   315→            append_run_log(
   316→                f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches} "
   317→                f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   318→            )
   319→
   320→    record_execution_issue = partial(_record_execution_issue, append_run_log)
   321→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   322→    summary_config = BatchRunSummaryConfig(
   323→        created_at=summary_created_at,
   324→        run_stamp=stamp,
   325→        runner=runner,
   326→        run_parallel=run_parallel,
   327→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   328→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   329→        max_parallel_batches=max_parallel_batches,
   330→        batch_timeout_seconds=batch_timeout_seconds,
   331→        batch_max_retries=batch_max_retries,
   332→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   333→        heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   334→        stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   335→        stall_kill_seconds=stall_kill_seconds,
   336→        immutable_packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   337→        prompt_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   338→        run_dir=run_dir,
   339→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   340→        run_log_path=run_log_path,
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    def write_run_summary(*, successful_batches, failed_batches, interrupted=False, interruption_reason=None):
   344→        _write_run_summary_impl(
   345→            summary_path=run_summary_path,
   346→            summary_config=summary_config,
   347→            batch_status=batch_status,
   348→            successful_batches=successful_batches,
   349→            failed_batches=failed_batches,
   350→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   351→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   352→            append_run_log_fn=append_run_log,
   353→            interrupted=interrupted,
   354→            interruption_reason=interruption_reason,
   355→        )
   356→
   357→    try:
   358→        execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   359→            tasks=tasks,
   360→            options=BatchExecutionOptions(
   361→                run_parallel=run_parallel,
   362→                max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   363→                heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   364→            ),
   365→            progress_fn=_report_progress,
   366→            error_log_fn=record_execution_issue,
   367→        )
   368→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   369→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   370→            key = str(idx + 1)
   371→            state = batch_status.setdefault(
   372→                key,
   373→                {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   374→            )
   375→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   376→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   377→        write_run_summary(
   378→            successful_batches=[],
   379→            failed_batches=[],
   380→            interrupted=True,
   381→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   382→        )
   383→        append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   384→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   385→
   386→    allowed_dims = {
   387→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   388→    }
   389→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   390→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   391→        failures=execution_failures,
   392→        output_files=output_files,
   393→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   394→    )
   395→
   396→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   397→    failure_set = set(failures)
   398→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   399→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   400→        key = str(idx + 1)
   401→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   402→            key,
   403→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   404→        )
   405→        if idx not in failure_set:
   406→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   407→            continue
   408→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   409→            state["status"] = "failed"
   410→            continue
   411→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   412→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   413→            continue
   414→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   415→
   416→    write_run_summary(
   417→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   418→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   419→    )
   420→
   421→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   422→        append_run_log(
   423→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   424→        )
   425→        print_failures_and_raise_fn(
   426→            failures=failures,
   427→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   428→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   429→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   430→        )
   431→    elif failures:
   432→        print(
   433→            colorize_fn(
   434→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   435→                "yellow",
   436→            )
   437→        )
   438→        print_failures_fn(
   439→            failures=failures,
   440→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   441→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   442→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   443→        )
   444→        append_run_log(
   445→            "run-partial "
   446→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   447→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   448→        )
   449→
   450→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   451→    reviewed_files = collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   452→        batches=batches,
   453→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   454→    )
   455→    full_sweep_included = any(
   456→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   457→        for idx in successful_indexes
   458→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   459→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   460→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   461→    )
   462→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   463→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   464→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   465→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   466→    }
   467→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   468→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   469→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   470→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   471→    if reviewed_files:
   472→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   473→        print(
   474→            colorize_fn(
   475→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   476→                "dim",
   477→            )
   478→        )
   479→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   480→        runner=runner,
   481→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   482→        run_stamp=stamp,
   483→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   484→    )
   485→    merged_assessment_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   486→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   487→    )
   488→    merged_issue_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   489→        [
   490→            issue.get("dimension")
   491→            for issue in (merged.get("issues") or [])
   492→            if isinstance(issue, dict)
   493→        ]
   494→    )
   495→    merged_imported_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   496→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_issue_dims
   497→    )
   498→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   499→    missing_after_import = print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   500→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   501→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   502→        scan_path=scan_path,
   503→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   504→    )
   505→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   506→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   507→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   508→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   509→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   510→    }
   511→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   512→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   513→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   514→    print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   515→
   516→    try:
   517→        do_import_fn(
   518→            str(merged_path),
   519→            state,
   520→            lang,
   521→            state_file,
   522→            config=config,
   523→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   524→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   525→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   526→        )
   527→    except SystemExit as exc:
   528→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   529→        raise
   530→    except Exception as exc:
   531→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   532→        raise
   533→    append_run_log(
   534→        "run-finished "
   535→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   536→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   537→    )
   538→
   539→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   540→        followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   541→            lang_name=lang.name,
   542→            scan_path=str(args.path),
   543→        )
   544→        if followup_code != 0:
   545→            raise CommandError(
   546→                f"Error: follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   547→                exit_code=followup_code,
   548→            )
   549→
   550→
   551→__all__ = ["do_run_batches"]
   552→
```

```
    60→def _compute_ts_destructure_props(content, lines):
    61→    long_destructures = re.findall(r"\{\s*(\w+(?:\s*,\s*\w+){8,})\s*\}", content)
    62→    if not long_destructures:
    63→        return None
    64→    max_props = max(len(d.split(",")) for d in long_destructures)
    65→    return max_props, f"destructure w/{max_props} props"
    66→
    67→
    68→def _compute_ts_inline_types(content, lines):
    69→    inline_types = len(
    70→        re.findall(r"^(?:export\s+)?(?:type|interface)\s+\w+", content, re.MULTILINE)
    71→    )
    72→    if inline_types > 3:
    73→        return inline_types, f"{inline_types} inline types"
    74→    return None

   137→def phase_logs(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   138→    log_result = logs_detector_mod.detect_logs_result(path)
   139→    log_entries = log_result.entries
   140→    total_files = log_result.population_size
   141→    log_groups: dict[tuple, list] = defaultdict(list)
   142→    for e in log_entries:
   143→        log_groups[(e["file"], e["tag"])].append(e)
   144→    results = []
   145→    for (file, tag), entries in log_groups.items():
   146→        results.append(
   147→            make_issue(
   148→                "logs",
   149→                file,
   150→                tag,
   151→                tier=1,
   152→                confidence="high",
   153→                summary=f"{len(entries)} tagged logs [{tag}]",
   154→                detail={
   155→                    "count": len(entries),
   156→                    "lines": [e["line"] for e in entries[:20]],
   157→                },
   158→            )
   159→        )
   160→    log(f"         {len(log_entries)} instances → {len(results)} issues")
   161→    return results, {"logs": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files)}
   162→
   163→
   164→def phase_unused(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   165→    entries, total_files = unused_detector_mod.detect_unused(path)
   166→    return make_unused_issues(entries, log), {
   167→        "unused": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_files),
   168→    }
   169→
   170→
   171→def phase_exports(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   172→    export_entries, total_exports = exports_detector_mod.detect_dead_exports(path)
   173→    results = []
   174→    for e in export_entries:
   175→        results.append(
   176→            make_issue(
   177→                "exports",
   178→                e["file"],
   179→                e["name"],
   180→                tier=2,
   181→                confidence="high",
   182→                summary=f"Dead export: {e['name']}",
   183→                detail={"line": e.get("line"), "kind": e.get("kind")},
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→    log(f"         {len(export_entries)} instances → {len(results)} issues")
   187→    return results, {"exports": total_exports}

   190→def phase_deprecated(
   191→    path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract
   192→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   193→    dep_result = deprecated_detector_mod.detect_deprecated_result(path)
   194→    dep_entries = dep_result.entries
   195→    total_deprecated = dep_result.population_size
   196→    results = []
   197→    for e in dep_entries:
   198→        if e["kind"] == "property":
   199→            continue
   200→        tier = 1 if e["importers"] == 0 else 3
   201→        results.append(
   202→            make_issue(
   203→                "deprecated",
   204→                e["file"],
   205→                e["symbol"],
   206→                tier=tier,
   207→                confidence="high",
   208→                summary=f"Deprecated: {e['symbol']} ({e['importers']} importers)"
   209→                + (" → safe to delete" if e["importers"] == 0 else ""),
   210→                detail={"importers": e["importers"], "line": e["line"]},
   211→            )
   212→        )
   213→    log(
   214→        f"         {len(dep_entries)} instances → {len(results)} issues (properties suppressed)"
   215→    )
   216→    return results, {"deprecated": total_deprecated}

   219→def phase_structural(

   221→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   222→    structural: dict[str, dict] = {}
   223→
   224→    large_entries, file_count = large_detector_mod.detect_large_files(

   273→
   274→    # Flat directories (too many files → missing sub-organization)
   275→    flat_entries, dir_count = flat_dirs_detector_mod.detect_flat_dirs(
   276→        path,
   277→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   278→        config=flat_dirs_detector_mod.FlatDirDetectionConfig(),
   279→    )
   280→    for e in flat_entries:
   281→        child_dir_count = int(e.get("child_dir_count", 0))
   282→        combined_score = int(e.get("combined_score", e.get("file_count", 0)))
   283→        results.append(
   284→            make_issue(
   285→                "flat_dirs",
   286→                e["directory"],
   287→                "",
   288→                tier=3,
   289→                confidence="medium",
   290→                summary=flat_dirs_detector_mod.format_flat_dir_summary(e),
   291→                detail={
   292→                    "file_count": e["file_count"],
   293→                    "child_dir_count": child_dir_count,
   294→                    "combined_score": combined_score,
   295→                    "kind": e.get("kind", "overload"),
   296→                    "parent_sibling_count": int(e.get("parent_sibling_count", 0)),
   297→                    "wrapper_item_count": int(e.get("wrapper_item_count", 0)),
   298→                    "sparse_child_count": int(e.get("sparse_child_count", 0)),
   299→                    "sparse_child_ratio": float(e.get("sparse_child_ratio", 0.0)),
   300→                    "sparse_child_file_threshold": int(
   301→                        e.get("sparse_child_file_threshold", 0)
   302→                    ),
   303→                },
   304→            )
   305→        )
   306→    if flat_entries:
   307→        log(
   308→            f"         flat dirs: {len(flat_entries)} overloaded directories "
   309→            "(files/subdirs/combined)"
   310→        )
   311→
   312→    # TS-specific: props bloat
   313→    props_thresh = lang.props_threshold
   314→    prop_entries, prop_count = props_detector_mod.detect_prop_interface_bloat(
   315→        path, threshold=props_thresh
   316→    )
   317→    for e in prop_entries:
   318→        pc = e["prop_count"]
   319→        # Tiered severity: 15-29=low, 30-49=medium, 50+=high
   320→        if pc >= 50:
   321→            conf, tier = "high", 4
   322→        elif pc >= 30:
   323→            conf, tier = "medium", 3
   324→        else:
   325→            conf, tier = "low", 3
   326→        results.append(
   327→            make_issue(
   328→                "props",
   329→                e["file"],
   330→                e["interface"],
   331→                tier=tier,
   332→                confidence=conf,
   333→                summary=f"Bloated {e.get('kind', 'props')}: {e['interface']} ({pc} fields)",
   334→                detail={
   335→                    "prop_count": pc,
   336→                    "line": e["line"],
   337→                    "kind": e.get("kind", "props"),
   338→                },
   339→            )
   340→        )
   341→
   342→    # TS-specific: passthrough components
   343→    pt_entries = detect_passthrough_components(path)
   344→    for e in pt_entries:
   345→        results.append(
   346→            make_issue(
   347→                "props",
   348→                e["file"],
   349→                f"passthrough::{e['component']}",
   350→                tier=e["tier"],
   351→                confidence=e["confidence"],
   352→                summary=f"Passthrough component: {e['component']} "
   353→                f"({e['passthrough']}/{e['total_props']} props forwarded, {e['ratio']:.0%})",
   354→                detail={
   355→                    "passthrough": e["passthrough"],
   356→                    "total_props": e["total_props"],
   357→                    "ratio": e["ratio"],
   358→                    "line": e["line"],
   359→                    "passthrough_props": e["passthrough_props"],
   360→                    "direct_props": e["direct_props"],
   361→                },
   362→            )
   363→        )
   364→    potentials = {
   365→        "structural": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, file_count),
   366→        "flat_dirs": dir_count,
   367→        "props": max(prop_count, len(pt_entries)) if prop_count else len(pt_entries),
   368→    }
   369→    return results, potentials
   370→
   371→
   372→def _make_boundary_issues(
   373→    single_entries: list[dict],
   374→    path: Path,
   375→    graph: dict,
   376→    lang: LangRuntimeContract,
   377→    shared_prefix: str,
   378→    tools_prefix: str,
   379→) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
   380→    """Create boundary-candidate issues, deduplicated against single-use."""
   381→    single_use_emitted = set()
   382→    for e in single_entries:
   383→        is_size_ok = 50 <= e["loc"] <= 200
   384→        is_colocated = lang.get_area and (
   385→            lang.get_area(rel(e["file"])) == lang.get_area(e["sole_importer"])
   386→        )
   387→        if not is_size_ok and not is_colocated:
   388→            single_use_emitted.add(rel(e["file"]))
   389→
   390→    results = []
   391→    deduped = 0
   392→    boundary_entries, total_shared = coupling_detector_mod.detect_boundary_candidates(
   393→        path,
   394→        graph,
   395→        shared_prefix=shared_prefix,
   396→        tools_prefix=tools_prefix,
   397→        skip_basenames={"index.ts", "index.tsx"},
   398→    )
   399→    for e in boundary_entries:
   400→        if rel(e["file"]) in single_use_emitted:
   401→            deduped += 1
   402→            continue
   403→        results.append(
   404→            make_issue(
   405→                "coupling",
   406→                e["file"],
   407→                f"boundary::{e['sole_tool']}",
   408→                tier=3,
   409→                confidence="medium",
   410→                summary=f"Boundary candidate ({e['loc']} LOC): only used by {e['sole_tool']} "
   411→                f"({e['importer_count']} importers)",
   412→                detail={
   413→                    "sole_tool": e["sole_tool"],
   414→                    "importer_count": e["importer_count"],
   415→                    "loc": e["loc"],
   416→                },
   417→            )
   418→        )
   419→    if deduped:
   420→        log(f"         ({deduped} boundary candidates skipped — covered by single_use)")
   421→    return results, total_shared
   422→
   423→
   424→def phase_coupling(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   425→    results = []
   426→    graph = deps_detector_mod.build_dep_graph(path)
   427→    lang.dep_graph = graph
   428→    zm = lang.zone_map
   429→
   430→    # Single-use (shared helper)
   431→    single_entries, single_candidates = (
   432→        single_use_detector_mod.detect_single_use_abstractions(
   433→            path, graph, barrel_names=lang.barrel_names
   434→        )
   435→    )
   436→    single_entries = filter_entries(zm, single_entries, "single_use")
   437→    results.extend(
   438→        make_single_use_issues(
   439→            single_entries, lang.get_area, skip_dir_names={"commands"}, stderr_fn=log
   440→        )
   441→    )
   442→    src_path = get_src_path()
   443→    shared_prefix = f"{src_path}/shared/"
   444→    tools_prefix = f"{src_path}/tools/"
   445→    coupling_entries, coupling_edge_counts = coupling_detector_mod.detect_coupling_violations(
   446→        path, graph, shared_prefix=shared_prefix, tools_prefix=tools_prefix
   447→    )
   448→    coupling_entries = filter_entries(zm, coupling_entries, "coupling")
   449→    for e in coupling_entries:
   450→        results.append(
   451→            make_issue(
   452→                "coupling",
   453→                e["file"],
   454→                e["target"],
   455→                tier=2,
   456→                confidence="high",
   457→                summary=f"Backwards coupling: shared imports {e['target']} (tool: {e['tool']})",
   458→                detail={
   459→                    "target": e["target"],
   460→                    "tool": e["tool"],
   461→                    "direction": e["direction"],
   462→                },
   463→            )
   464→        )
   465→
   466→    # TS-specific: boundary candidates (deduplicated against single-use)
   467→    boundary_issues, _ = _make_boundary_issues(
   468→        single_entries, path, graph, lang, shared_prefix, tools_prefix
   469→    )
   470→    results.extend(boundary_issues)
   471→
   472→    # TS-specific: cross-tool imports
   473→    cross_tool, cross_edge_counts = coupling_detector_mod.detect_cross_tool_imports(
   474→        path, graph, tools_prefix=tools_prefix
   475→    )
   476→    cross_tool = filter_entries(zm, cross_tool, "coupling")
   477→    for e in cross_tool:
   478→        results.append(
   479→            make_issue(
   480→                "coupling",
   481→                e["file"],
   482→                e["target"],
   483→                tier=2,
   484→                confidence="high",
   485→                summary=f"Cross-tool import: {e['source_tool']}→{e['target_tool']} ({e['target']})",
   486→                detail={
   487→                    "target": e["target"],
   488→                    "source_tool": e["source_tool"],
   489→                    "target_tool": e["target_tool"],
   490→                    "direction": e["direction"],
   491→                },
   492→            )
   493→        )
   494→    if cross_tool:
   495→        log(f"         cross-tool: {len(cross_tool)} imports")
   496→
   497→    # Cycles + orphaned (shared helpers)
   498→    cycle_entries, _ = graph_detector_mod.detect_cycles(graph)
   499→    cycle_entries = filter_entries(zm, cycle_entries, "cycles", file_key="files")
   500→    results.extend(make_cycle_issues(cycle_entries, log))
   501→    orphan_entries, total_graph_files = orphaned_detector_mod.detect_orphaned_files(
   502→        path,
   503→        graph,
   504→        extensions=lang.extensions,
   505→        options=orphaned_detector_mod.OrphanedDetectionOptions(
   506→            extra_entry_patterns=lang.entry_patterns,
   507→            extra_barrel_names=lang.barrel_names,
   508→            dynamic_import_finder=deps_detector_mod.build_dynamic_import_targets,
   509→            alias_resolver=deps_detector_mod.ts_alias_resolver,
   510→        ),
   511→    )
   512→    orphan_entries = filter_entries(zm, orphan_entries, "orphaned")
   513→    results.extend(make_orphaned_issues(orphan_entries, log))
   514→
   515→    # Re-export facades (shared detector)
   516→    facade_entries, _ = facade_detector_mod.detect_reexport_facades(graph)
   517→    facade_entries = filter_entries(zm, facade_entries, "facade")
   518→    results.extend(make_facade_issues(facade_entries, log))
   519→
   520→    # TS-specific: pattern consistency
   521→    pattern_result = patterns_detector_mod.detect_pattern_anomalies_result(path)
   522→    pattern_entries = pattern_result.entries
   523→    total_areas = pattern_result.population_size
   524→    for e in pattern_entries:
   525→        results.append(
   526→            make_issue(
   527→                "patterns",
   528→                e["area"],
   529→                e["family"],
   530→                tier=3,
   531→                confidence=e.get("confidence", "low"),
   532→                summary=f"Competing patterns ({e['family']}): {e['review'][:120]}",
   533→                detail={
   534→                    "family": e["family"],
   535→                    "patterns_used": e["patterns_used"],
   536→                    "pattern_count": e["pattern_count"],
   537→                    "review": e["review"],
   538→                },
   539→            )
   540→        )
   541→
   542→    # TS-specific: naming consistency
   543→    naming_entries, total_dirs = naming_detector_mod.detect_naming_inconsistencies(
   544→        path,
   545→        file_finder=lang.file_finder,
   546→        skip_names=TS_SKIP_NAMES,
   547→        skip_dirs=TS_SKIP_DIRS,
   548→    )
   549→    for e in naming_entries:
   550→        results.append(
   551→            make_issue(
   552→                "naming",
   553→                e["directory"],
   554→                e["minority"],
   555→                tier=3,
   556→                confidence="low",
   557→                summary=f"Naming inconsistency: {e['minority_count']} {e['minority']} files "
   558→                f"in {e['majority']}-majority dir ({e['total_files']} total)",
   559→                detail={
   560→                    "majority": e["majority"],
   561→                    "majority_count": e["majority_count"],
   562→                    "minority": e["minority"],
   563→                    "minority_count": e["minority_count"],
   564→                    "outliers": e["outliers"],
   565→                },
   566→            )
   567→        )
   568→    log(f"         → {len(results)} coupling/structural issues total")
   569→    coupling_edges = coupling_edge_counts.eligible_edges
   570→    cross_edges = cross_edge_counts.eligible_edges
   571→    potentials = {
   572→        "single_use": adjust_potential(zm, single_candidates),
   573→        "coupling": coupling_edges + cross_edges,
   574→        "cycles": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   575→        "orphaned": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   576→        "patterns": total_areas,
   577→        "naming": total_dirs,
   578→        "facade": adjust_potential(zm, total_graph_files),
   579→    }
   580→    return results, potentials
   581→
   582→
   583→def phase_smells(path: Path, lang: LangRuntimeContract) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   584→    smell_entries, total_smell_files = smells_detector_mod.detect_smells(path)
   585→    results = make_smell_issues(smell_entries, log)
   586→
   587→    # TS-specific: React state sync anti-patterns
   588→    react_entries, total_effects = react_detector_mod.detect_state_sync(path)
   589→    for e in react_entries:
   590→        setter_str = ", ".join(e["setters"])
   591→        results.append(
   592→            make_issue(
   593→                "react",
   594→                e["file"],
   595→                setter_str,
   596→                tier=3,
   597→                confidence="medium",
   598→                summary=f"State sync anti-pattern: useEffect only calls {setter_str}",
   599→                detail={"line": e["line"], "setters": e["setters"]},
   600→            )
   601→        )
   602→    if react_entries:
   603→        log(f"         react: {len(react_entries)} state sync anti-patterns")
   604→
   605→    # TS-specific: Context provider nesting depth
   606→    nesting_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_context_nesting(path)
   607→    for e in nesting_entries:
   608→        providers_str = " → ".join(e["providers"][:5])
   609→        results.append(
   610→            make_issue(
   611→                "react",
   612→                e["file"],
   613→                f"nesting::{e['depth']}",
   614→                tier=3,
   615→                confidence="medium",
   616→                summary=f"Deep provider nesting ({e['depth']} levels): {providers_str}",
   617→                detail={"depth": e["depth"], "providers": e["providers"]},
   618→            )
   619→        )
   620→    if nesting_entries:
   621→        log(f"         react: {len(nesting_entries)} deep provider nesting")
   622→
   623→    # TS-specific: Hook return bloat
   624→    hook_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_hook_return_bloat(path)
   625→    for e in hook_entries:
   626→        results.append(
   627→            make_issue(
   628→                "react",
   629→                e["file"],
   630→                f"hook_bloat::{e['hook']}",
   631→                tier=3,
   632→                confidence="medium",
   633→                summary=f"Hook return bloat: {e['hook']} returns {e['field_count']} fields",
   634→                detail={
   635→                    "hook": e["hook"],
   636→                    "field_count": e["field_count"],
   637→                    "line": e["line"],
   638→                },
   639→            )
   640→        )
   641→    if hook_entries:
   642→        log(f"         react: {len(hook_entries)} bloated hook returns")
   643→
   644→    # TS-specific: Boolean state explosion
   645→    bool_entries, _ = react_detector_mod.detect_boolean_state_explosion(path)
   646→    for e in bool_entries:
   647→        states_str = ", ".join(e["states"][:5])
   648→        results.append(
   649→            make_issue(
   650→                "react",
   651→                e["file"],
   652→                f"bool_state::{e['prefix']}",
   653→                tier=3,
   654→                confidence="low",
   655→                summary=f"Boolean state explosion: {e['count']} boolean useState hooks ({states_str})",
   656→                detail={
   657→                    "count": e["count"],
   658→                    "setters": e["setters"],
   659→                    "states": e["states"],
   660→                    "line": e["line"],
   661→                },
   662→            )
   663→        )
   664→    if bool_entries:
   665→        log(f"         react: {len(bool_entries)} boolean state explosions")
   666→
   667→    return results, {
   668→        "smells": adjust_potential(lang.zone_map, total_smell_files),
   669→        "react": total_effects,
   670→    }
   671→
```

```
1→"""Reflect-stage dashboard rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.epic_triage import detect_recurring_patterns
     7→
     8→
     9→def _print_reflect_dashboard(si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
    10→    """Show completed clusters, resolved issues, and recurring patterns."""
    11→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
    12→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_issues", {})
    13→    open_issues = getattr(si, "open_issues", {})
    14→
    15→    _print_completed_clusters(completed)
    16→    _print_resolved_issues(resolved)
    17→    recurring = _print_recurring_patterns(open_issues, resolved)
    18→    if not recurring and not completed and not resolved:
    19→        print(colorize("\n  First triage — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
    20→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
    21→        print(
    22→            colorize(
    23→                "  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?",
    24→                "dim",
    25→            )
    26→        )
    27→        print(colorize("  - Which issues will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
    28→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _print_completed_clusters(completed: list[dict]) -> None:
    32→    """Print completed cluster context for reflect stage."""
    33→    if not completed:
    34→        return
    35→    print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
    36→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
    37→        name = cluster.get("name", "?")
    38→        count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    39→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
    40→        print(f"    {name}: {count} issues")
    41→        if thesis:
    42→            print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
    43→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
    44→            print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
    45→    if len(completed) > 10:
    46→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
    47→
    48→
    49→def _print_resolved_issues(resolved: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
    50→    """Print resolved issues delta since last triage."""
    51→    if not resolved:
    52→        return
    53→    print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved issues since last triage: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
    54→    for issue_id, issue in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
    55→        status = issue.get("status", "")
    56→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    57→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    58→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
    59→        print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
    60→        print(colorize(f"      {issue_id}", "dim"))
    61→    if len(resolved) > 10:
    62→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _print_recurring_patterns(open_issues: dict, resolved: dict[str, dict]) -> bool:
    66→    """Print recurring pattern diagnostics for reflect stage."""
    67→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(open_issues, resolved)
    68→    if not recurring:
    69→        return False
    70→    print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
    71→    for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
    72→        resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
    73→        open_count = len(info["open"])
    74→        label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
    75→        print(
    76→            colorize(
    77→                f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open \u2014 {label}",
    78→                "yellow",
    79→            )
    80→        )
    81→    return True
    82→
```
